    create table auction_items (
        current_price float(53),
        release_year integer,
        starting_price float(53),
        created_at timestamp(6),
        id bigint generated by default as identity,
        seller_id bigint,
        updated_at timestamp(6),
        item_type varchar(31) not null,
        artist varchar(255),
        brand varchar(255),
        description varchar(255),
        manufacturer varchar(255),
        medium varchar(255),
        model_name varchar(255),
        name varchar(255),
        primary key (id)
    )
Hibernate: 
    create table auction_sessions (
        bid_count integer,
        current_price numeric(15,2),
        starting_price numeric(15,2),
        created_at timestamp(6),
        end_time timestamp(6),
        id bigint generated by default as identity,
        item_id bigint unique,
        seller_id bigint,
        start_time timestamp(6),
        updated_at timestamp(6),
        version bigint,
        winner_id bigint,
        category varchar(60),
        title varchar(200),
        description varchar(1000),
        status varchar(255) check (status in ('PENDING','OPEN','RUNNING','FINISHED','PAID','CANCELED')),
        primary key (id)
    )

    create table auction_users (

        password varchar(255),
